## Verification Logic

Based on the extracted text, the following calculations are recommended:

1. **Local Mean Time (LMT)**: 
   `LMT = Standard Time + (Longitude_Place - Longitude_Standard)*4 minutes`

2. **Sidereal Time Calculation**: 
   `Sidereal_Time_Birth = Sidereal_Time_Noon + Interval + Correction(Longitude)`

3. **Ayanamsa**: 
   `Sidereal_Longitude = Tropical_Longitude - Ayanamsa` (Use Lahiri for standard KP).
